// ===================== 狀態 =====================
const KEY = 'swsh-ai-island-v1';
let state = { char: 'xiaosi', visited: [], seen: false };
try { const s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || 'null'); if (s && CHARS[s.char]) state = Object.assign(state, s); } catch (e) {}
const save = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(state)); } catch (e) {} };
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, m => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[m]));
const reduceMotion = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

function toast(msg) {
  const t = $('#toast'); t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(toast.h); toast.h = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2200);
}

// ===================== 介面：快捷列 / 開場 =====================
const dock = $('#dock');
PAV.forEach(p => {
  const b = document.createElement('button');
  b.type = 'button'; b.dataset.id = p.id; b.setAttribute('aria-label', `${p.name}（${p.ride}）`);
  b.innerHTML = `<span class="ic" style="background:${p.s};border:2px solid ${p.c}">${p.emoji}</span><span class="nm">${p.name}</span>`;
  b.addEventListener('click', () => goTo(p.id));
  dock.appendChild(b);
  const f = document.createElement('button');
  f.type = 'button'; f.style.setProperty('--c', p.c);
  f.innerHTML = `<span>${p.emoji}</span>${p.name}`;
  f.addEventListener('click', () => openPanel(p.id));
  $('#fb-grid').appendChild(f);
});
function markVisited() { dock.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('visited', state.visited.includes(b.dataset.id))); }
markVisited();

const pick = $('#pick');
Object.entries(CHARS).forEach(([id, c]) => {
  const b = document.createElement('button');
  b.type = 'button'; b.dataset.id = id;
  b.style.setProperty('--c', c.c); b.style.setProperty('--s', c.s); b.style.setProperty('--i', c.i);
  b.innerHTML = `<img src="${IMG[id]}" alt=""><b>${c.name}</b><span>${c.trait}</span>`;
  b.setAttribute('aria-label', `選擇 ${c.name}（${c.trait}）`);
  b.addEventListener('click', () => { state.char = id; save(); refreshPick(); applyChar(); });
  pick.appendChild(b);
});
function refreshPick() { pick.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.id === state.char)); }
refreshPick();
$('#go').addEventListener('click', () => { $('#intro').hidden = true; state.seen = true; save(); toast(`和 ${CHARS[state.char].name} 一起出發！`); });
$('#t-char').addEventListener('click', () => { $('#intro').hidden = false; });
if (state.seen) $('#intro').hidden = true;

// ===================== 指令館視窗 =====================
const panel = $('#panel'), pc = $('#pc');
function renderPrompt(text) {
  return esc(text).replace(/【([^】]+)】/g, (m, label) => {
    const w = Math.min(Math.max(label.length * 1.1 + 2, 5), 26);
    return `<input type="text" data-label="${label}" placeholder="${label}" style="width:${w}em" aria-label="${label}">`;
  });
}
function promptText(box) {
  let out = '';
  box.childNodes.forEach(n => {
    if (n.nodeType === 3) out += n.textContent;
    else if (n.tagName === 'INPUT') out += n.value.trim() ? n.value.trim() : `【${n.dataset.label}】`;
    else out += n.textContent;
  });
  return out;
}
async function copyText(text) {
  try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); return true; } catch (e) {
    const ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = text; ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0';
    document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); let ok = false; try { ok = document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (e2) {}
    ta.remove(); return ok;
  }
}
function openPanel(id) {
  if (id === 'rules') return openRules();
  const p = byId[id], g = CHARS[p.guide];
  const idx = PAV.indexOf(p), next = PAV[(idx + 1) % PAV.length];
  pc.style.setProperty('--c', p.c); pc.style.setProperty('--s', p.s); pc.style.setProperty('--i', p.i);
  pc.innerHTML = `
    <div class="ph"><span class="ic">${p.emoji}</span><div><small>${p.ride}・館長 ${g.name}</small><h2 id="p-title">${p.name}</h2></div><button class="x" type="button" aria-label="關閉">✕</button></div>
    <div class="pb">
      <div class="guide" style="--gc:${g.c};--gi:${g.i}"><img src="${IMG[p.guide]}" alt="${g.name}"><div class="bub"><b>${g.name}：</b>${p.line}</div></div>
      <p style="margin:0 0 10px;font-size:14.5px">${p.intro}</p>
      <div class="steps"><span>① 填入框框</span><span>② 複製指令</span><span>③ 貼到 Gemini 或其他 AI</span></div>
      ${p.extra ? `<div class="extra">${p.extra.map(x => `<a href="${x.href}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><span>${x.e}</span><span><b>${x.t}</b><small>${x.d}</small></span></a>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
      ${p.prompts.map((q, i) => `
        <div class="card">
          <h3><span class="n">${i + 1}</span>${q.t}</h3>
          <div class="use">🕐 適合：${q.use}</div>
          <div class="prompt">${renderPrompt(q.p)}</div>
          <div class="acts"><button class="btn copy" type="button">📋 複製指令</button><button class="btn ghost clr" type="button">↺ 清空</button></div>
        </div>`).join('')}
      <div class="warn">⚠️ AI 的回答可能出錯，請查證後再使用；繳交的作品要是你自己完成的，並註明哪裡用了 AI。</div>
    </div>
    <div class="pf"><a class="btn ai" href="${GEMINI}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">✨ 開啟 Gemini</a><button class="btn next" type="button">下一館：${next.emoji} ${next.name} →</button></div>`;
  pc.querySelector('.x').addEventListener('click', closePanel);
  pc.querySelector('.next').addEventListener('click', () => { closePanel(); setTimeout(() => goTo(next.id), 150); });
  pc.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(card => {
    const box = card.querySelector('.prompt');
    card.querySelector('.copy').addEventListener('click', async e => {
      const btn = e.currentTarget;
      const empty = [...box.querySelectorAll('input')].filter(x => !x.value.trim()).length;
      const ok = await copyText(promptText(box));
      if (ok) {
        btn.classList.add('ok'); btn.textContent = '✅ 已複製！';
        setTimeout(() => { btn.classList.remove('ok'); btn.textContent = '📋 複製指令'; }, 1800);
        toast(empty ? `已複製！還有 ${empty} 個【】可以貼上後再補` : '已複製！貼到 Gemini 就能用 ✨');
      } else toast('複製失敗，請手動選取文字');
    });
    card.querySelector('.clr').addEventListener('click', () => box.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(x => (x.value = '')));
  });
  if (!state.visited.includes(id)) { state.visited.push(id); save(); markVisited(); }
  if (!panel.open) panel.showModal();
  pc.querySelector('.pb').scrollTop = 0;
}
function openRules() {
  pc.style.setProperty('--c', '#5BB334'); pc.style.setProperty('--s', '#F0FDF4'); pc.style.setProperty('--i', '#166534');
  pc.innerHTML = `
    <div class="ph"><span class="ic">📜</span><div><small>進島前請先讀一讀</small><h2 id="p-title">AI 使用公約</h2></div><button class="x" type="button" aria-label="關閉">✕</button></div>
    <div class="pb">
      <ul class="rulelist">
        <li>🤝 <b>AI 是學伴，不是代筆</b>：用 AI 幫你發想、整理、檢查，但計畫、紀錄與成果要自己完成，不直接繳交 AI 產生的內容。</li>
        <li>🔍 <b>查證再使用</b>：AI 可能說錯，甚至編造不存在的書名、數據或網址。重要資訊請到 WebPAC、圖書館或可信網站確認。</li>
        <li>🏷️ <b>誠實標註</b>：在計畫、紀錄或成果中說明哪裡用了 AI、用了哪個工具、怎麼使用。</li>
        <li>🔒 <b>保護個資</b>：不要輸入自己或他人的姓名、電話、地址、照片等個人資料。</li>
        <li>📏 <b>遵守規定</b>：請依學校規範與各 AI 平台的使用條款（含年齡限制）使用。</li>
      </ul>
      <div class="warn">有疑問嗎？歡迎到圖書資訊中心找老師聊聊：(03) 856-1455 轉 517</div>
    </div>
    <div class="pf"><span></span><button class="btn next" type="button">我知道了 👍</button></div>`;
  pc.querySelector('.x').addEventListener('click', closePanel);
  pc.querySelector('.next').addEventListener('click', closePanel);
  if (!panel.open) panel.showModal();
}
function closePanel() { if (panel.open) panel.close(); }
panel.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === panel) closePanel(); });
$('#t-rules').addEventListener('click', () => openPanel('rules'));

// ===================== 音樂（WebAudio 音樂盒） =====================
let audio = null, musicOn = false, musicTimer = null;
const MELODY = [72, 76, 79, 76, 74, 77, 81, 77, 72, 76, 79, 84, 83, 79, 76, 74];
const BASS = [48, 53, 48, 55];
function startMusic() {
  audio = audio || new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
  audio.resume();
  let step = 0; const beat = 0.32;
  const note = (m, t, len, vol, type) => {
    const o = audio.createOscillator(), g = audio.createGain();
    o.type = type; o.frequency.value = 440 * Math.pow(2, (m - 69) / 12);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t); g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol, t + 0.02); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + len);
    o.connect(g).connect(audio.destination); o.start(t); o.stop(t + len + 0.05);
  };
  let next = audio.currentTime + 0.05;
  musicTimer = setInterval(() => {
    while (next < audio.currentTime + 0.4) {
      note(MELODY[step % MELODY.length], next, beat * 1.8, 0.07, 'triangle');
      if (step % 4 === 0) note(BASS[(step / 4) % BASS.length], next, beat * 4, 0.05, 'sine');
      step++; next += beat;
    }
  }, 100);
}
$('#t-music').addEventListener('click', e => {
  musicOn = !musicOn; e.currentTarget.setAttribute('aria-pressed', musicOn);
  if (musicOn) startMusic(); else { clearInterval(musicTimer); if (audio) audio.suspend(); }
});
$('#t-full').addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (!document.fullscreenElement) document.documentElement.requestFullscreen && document.documentElement.requestFullscreen().catch(() => {});
  else document.exitFullscreen && document.exitFullscreen();
});

// ===================== 3D 場景 =====================

try {
  renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
} catch (e) { renderer = null; }
let goTo = id => openPanel(id);
let applyChar = () => { $('#joy-img').src = IMG[state.char]; };
applyChar();
if (!renderer || typeof THREE === 'undefined') { document.body.classList.add('no3d'); return; }

const host = $('#scene');
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 1.75));
renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
host.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

const scene = new THREE.Scene();
const DAY = { sky: 0xDCEFFB, fog: 0xE6F3FB }, NIGHT = { sky: 0x1E2650, fog: 0x232C5C };
scene.background = new THREE.Color(DAY.sky);
scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(DAY.fog, 120, 260);
const narrow = () => innerWidth < innerHeight;
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(narrow() ? 60 : 48, innerWidth / innerHeight, 1, 600);

const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xffffff, 0x9CC98A, 0.52); scene.add(hemi);
const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xFFF6E5, 0.62);
sun.position.set(40, 70, 30); sun.castShadow = true;
sun.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048);
Object.assign(sun.shadow.camera, { left: -75, right: 75, top: 75, bottom: -75, near: 10, far: 200 });
sun.shadow.bias = -0.0005; sun.shadow.normalBias = 0.06;
scene.add(sun);

const M = (color, opt = {}) => new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial(Object.assign({ color }, opt));
function mesh(geo, mat, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0, cast = true) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat); m.position.set(x, y, z); m.castShadow = cast; m.receiveShadow = true; return m;
}
const animators = [];
const put = (parent, child) => { parent.add(child); return child; };

// --- 地形 ---
const ocean = mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(400, 64), M(0xA9DCF2), 0, -0.8, 0, false); ocean.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; scene.add(ocean);
scene.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(66, 68, 1.2, 72), M(0xF6E5B8), 0, -0.85, 0, false));
const island = mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(61, 63, 1.4, 72), M(0xC5E8A7), 0, -0.7, 0, false); scene.add(island);
const pathMat = M(0xF7E8CB, { polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -4, polygonOffsetUnits: -4 });
const ring = mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(19, 24.5, 72), pathMat, 0, 0.08, 0, false); ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; scene.add(ring);
const plaza = mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(11, 48), pathMat, 0, 0.1, 0, false); plaza.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; scene.add(plaza);
const gateRoad = mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(6, 36), pathMat, 0, 0.12, 41, false); gateRoad.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; scene.add(gateRoad);
for (let a = 0; a < 4; a++) { const sp = mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(4, 9), pathMat, 0, 0.022, 0, false); sp.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; sp.rotation.z = a * Math.PI / 2; sp.position.set(Math.sin(a * Math.PI / 2) * 15, 0.09, Math.cos(a * Math.PI / 2) * 15); scene.add(sp); }

// --- 指令館位置 ---
const R = 37;
PAV.forEach((p, i) => {
  const a = (-150 + i * 300 / (PAV.length - 1)) * Math.PI / 180;
  p.pos = new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(a) * R, 0, -Math.cos(a) * R);
  p.door = new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(a) * (R - 10.5), 0, -Math.cos(a) * (R - 10.5));
  p.angle = a;
  const sp = mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(4.5, 10), pathMat, Math.sin(a) * 28.5, 0.07, -Math.cos(a) * 28.5, false);
  sp.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; sp.rotation.z = -a; scene.add(sp);
});

// --- 建築 ---
const white = M(0xFFFBF5), wood = M(0xC89B6D), metal = M(0xD6DDE8);
const windowMat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: 0x8FA6C8, emissive: 0xFFD27A, emissiveIntensity: 0 });
function pad(g, hex) { g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(8.2, 8.6, 0.5, 40), M(0xFFF8EC), 0, 0.25, 0, false)); g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(7.6, 7.6, 0.56, 40), M(hex), 0, 0.28, 0, false)); }
const B = {
  ferris(p) {
    const g = new THREE.Group(); pad(g, p.hex);
    [-1.6, 1.6].forEach(z => {
      [-1, 1].forEach(s => { const leg = mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.5, 11, 0.5), metal, s * 2.6, 5.6, z); leg.rotation.z = s * 0.24; g.add(leg); });
    });
    const wheel = new THREE.Group(); wheel.position.y = 10.4; g.add(wheel);
    const rim = M(0xF472B6);
    [-1.5, 1.5].forEach(z => { const t = mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(6.4, 0.22, 8, 48), rim, 0, 0, z); wheel.add(t); });
    put(wheel, mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.7, 0.7, 3.6, 16), M(0xFACC15), 0, 0, 0)).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    const cabins = [];
    const cols = [0xF9A8D4, 0x93C5FD, 0xFDE68A, 0x86EFAC, 0xC4B5FD, 0xFDBA74, 0x67E8F9, 0xFCA5A5];
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
      const a = k / 8 * Math.PI * 2;
      const sp = mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.18, 6.4, 0.18), metal, Math.cos(a) * 3.2, Math.sin(a) * 3.2, 0); sp.rotation.z = a - Math.PI / 2; wheel.add(sp);
      const cab = new THREE.Group(); cab.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 6.4, Math.sin(a) * 6.4, 0);
      cab.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.95, 0.75, 1.3, 12), M(cols[k]), 0, -1.1, 0));
      cab.add(mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.05, 0.7, 12), white, 0, -0.1, 0));
      wheel.add(cab); cabins.push(cab);
    }
    animators.push(dt => { wheel.rotation.z += dt * 0.25; cabins.forEach(c => (c.rotation.z = -wheel.rotation.z)); });
    p.h = 19; return g;
  },
  castle(p) {
    const g = new THREE.Group(); pad(g, p.hex);
    const wall = M(0xFFF4F7), roofA = M(0xA78BFA), roofB = M(0xF9A8D4);
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(9, 7, 6), wall, 0, 3.8, 0));
    for (let k = -3; k <= 3; k += 1.5) g.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.9, 0.9, 0.9), wall, k, 7.7, 2.6));
    [[-4.5, -3], [4.5, -3], [-4.5, 3], [4.5, 3]].forEach(([x, z], k) => {
      g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.3, 1.4, 9.5, 20), wall, x, 5, z));
      g.add(mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.8, 3.6, 20), k % 2 ? roofB : roofA, x, 11.5, z));
    });
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2, 2.2, 13, 24), wall, 0, 7, -0.5));
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(2.8, 5.5, 24), roofA, 0, 16.2, -0.5));
    const flag = mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.6, 0.9, 0.08), M(0xFACC15), 0.85, 19.6, -0.5); g.add(flag);
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.06, 0.06, 2.4, 6), metal, 0, 19.4, -0.5));
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4, 3.6, 0.3), M(0xA16207), 0, 2.3, 3.05));
    put(g, mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.2, 1.2, 0.3, 20, 1, false, 0, Math.PI), M(0xA16207), 0, 4.1, 3.05)).rotation.set(Math.PI / 2, 0, Math.PI / 2);
    [[-2.6, 5.2], [2.6, 5.2], [0, 9.8], [-1, 12.5], [1, 12.5]].forEach(([x, y]) => g.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.8, 1.2, 0.2), windowMat, x, y, y > 9 ? 1.5 : 3.02)));
    animators.push((dt, t) => { flag.rotation.y = Math.sin(t * 3) * 0.25; });
    p.h = 22; return g;
  },
  tower(p) {
    const g = new THREE.Group(); pad(g, p.hex);
    const bands = [0xF9A8D4, 0x93C5FD, 0xFDE68A, 0x86EFAC, 0xC4B5FD];
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(3.4, 3.8, 3, 28), white, 0, 1.5, 0));
    for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
      const y = 3 + k * 2.4;
      g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(3.0 - k * 0.15, 3.1 - k * 0.15, 2.0, 28), M(bands[k]), 0, y + 1, 0));
      g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(3.2 - k * 0.15, 3.2 - k * 0.15, 0.4, 28), white, 0, y + 2.2, 0));
    }
    put(g, mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(3.4, 0.18, 6, 32), wood, 0, 9.8, 0)).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(3.6, 5, 28), M(0x60A5FA), 0, 17.4, 0));
    const book = new THREE.Group(); book.position.y = 21.6; g.add(book);
    put(book, mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.2, 0.35, 1.6), M(0xF87171), -0.55, 0, 0)).rotation.z = 0.35;
    put(book, mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.2, 0.35, 1.6), M(0x60A5FA), 0.55, 0, 0)).rotation.z = -0.35;
    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) { const a = k / 6 * Math.PI * 2; put(g, mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.9, 1.3, 0.2), windowMat, Math.sin(a) * 2.95, 6.2 + (k % 2) * 4.8, Math.cos(a) * 2.95)).rotation.y = a; }
    animators.push((dt, t) => { book.rotation.y += dt * 0.8; book.position.y = 21.6 + Math.sin(t * 2) * 0.3; });
    p.h = 25; return g;
  },
  carousel(p) {
    const g = new THREE.Group(); pad(g, p.hex);
    const spin = new THREE.Group(); g.add(spin);
    spin.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(6, 6.2, 0.7, 36), M(0xFFF1E0), 0, 0.9, 0));
    spin.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.7, 0.7, 7, 16), M(0xFACC15), 0, 4.5, 0));
    const cols = [0xFB923C, 0xFFFFFF];
    for (let k = 0; k < 16; k++) put(spin, mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(6.8, 3.2, 1, 1, false, k / 16 * Math.PI * 2, Math.PI * 2 / 16), M(cols[k % 2]), 0, 9.6, 0)).geometry;
    spin.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(6.8, 6.8, 0.8, 32), M(0xFDBA74), 0, 7.6, 0));
    spin.add(mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.6, 12, 12), M(0xFACC15), 0, 11.5, 0));
    const horses = [];
    const hc = [0xFFFFFF, 0xF9A8D4, 0x93C5FD, 0xFDE68A, 0xC4B5FD, 0x86EFAC];
    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
      const a = k / 6 * Math.PI * 2, x = Math.cos(a) * 4.3, z = Math.sin(a) * 4.3;
      spin.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.1, 0.1, 6.4, 6), M(0xFACC15), x, 4.4, z));
      const h = new THREE.Group(); h.position.set(x, 3, z); h.rotation.y = -a;
      h.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.9, 0.9, 2), M(hc[k]), 0, 0, 0));
      h.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.7, 1.2, 0.7), M(hc[k]), 0, 0.7, 1));
      h.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.25, 0.8, 0.25), M(hc[k]), 0.3, -0.7, 0.7));
      h.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.25, 0.8, 0.25), M(hc[k]), -0.3, -0.7, -0.7));
      spin.add(h); horses.push(h);
    }
    animators.push((dt, t) => { spin.rotation.y += dt * 0.45; horses.forEach((h, k) => (h.position.y = 3 + Math.sin(t * 2.5 + k) * 0.5)); });
    p.h = 15; return g;
  },
  train(p) {
    const g = new THREE.Group(); pad(g, p.hex);
    const track = mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(5.6, 0.2, 6, 48), wood, 0, 0.75, 0, false); track.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; g.add(track);
    const st = new THREE.Group(); g.add(st);
    st.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.6, 0.4, 2.6), white, 0, 0.8, 0));
    [[-1.5, -1], [1.5, -1], [-1.5, 1], [1.5, 1]].forEach(([x, z]) => st.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.15, 0.15, 3.2, 8), white, x, 2.4, z)));
    put(st, mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(2.8, 1.6, 4), M(0x34D399), 0, 4.8, 0)).rotation.y = Math.PI / 4;
    const cars = [], cc = [0xF87171, 0xFACC15, 0x60A5FA, 0xC4B5FD];
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
      const car = new THREE.Group();
      if (k === 0) {
        car.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 1.2, 2.2), M(0x34D399), 0, 1.6, 0));
        put(car, mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.6, 1.6, 12), M(0x10B981), 0, 1.7, 1.2)).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
        car.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.22, 0.32, 0.9, 10), M(0x334155), 0, 2.7, 1.6));
        car.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.5, 0.3, 1.2), M(0xF87171), 0, 2.4, -0.4));
      } else {
        car.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 1, 1.9), M(cc[k]), 0, 1.5, 0));
      }
      [-0.7, 0.7].forEach(z => [-0.75, 0.75].forEach(x => put(car, mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.32, 0.32, 0.2, 10), M(0x334155), x, 0.95, z)).rotation.z = Math.PI / 2));
      g.add(car); cars.push(car);
    }
    let ang = 0;
    animators.push(dt => {
      ang += dt * 0.5;
      cars.forEach((c, k) => { const a = ang - k * 0.42; c.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 5.6, 0, Math.sin(a) * 5.6); c.rotation.y = -a; });
    });
    p.h = 11; return g;
  },
  bumper(p) {
    const g = new THREE.Group(); pad(g, p.hex);
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(12, 0.3, 10), M(0xE0F2FE), 0, 0.7, 0, false));
    const rail = M(0x22D3EE);
    [[0, 5], [0, -5]].forEach(([x, z]) => g.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(12, 0.8, 0.3), rail, x, 1.1, z)));
    [[6, 0], [-6, 0]].forEach(([x, z]) => g.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.3, 0.8, 10), rail, x, 1.1, z)));
    [[-5.8, -4.8], [5.8, -4.8], [-5.8, 4.8], [5.8, 4.8]].forEach(([x, z]) => g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.2, 0.2, 7, 8), white, x, 4.2, z)));
    const roof = mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(13, 0.5, 11), M(0x67E8F9), 0, 7.8, 0); g.add(roof);
    for (let k = -5; k <= 5; k += 2.5) put(g, mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.9, 1.2, 4), M(k % 5 === 0 ? 0xFACC15 : 0xF472B6), k, 7, 5.6)).rotation.x = Math.PI;
    const sign = mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(5, 1.6, 0.3), white, 0, 9, 0); g.add(sign);
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.5, 12, 12), M(0x22D3EE), -1.2, 9, 0.2)); g.add(mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.5, 12, 12), M(0x22D3EE), 1.2, 9, 0.2));
    const cars = [], cc = [0xF87171, 0xFACC15, 0x60A5FA, 0xA78BFA];
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
      const car = new THREE.Group();
      car.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.1, 1.1, 0.6, 18), M(0x334155), 0, 1.1, 0));
      car.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 0.8, 1.6), M(cc[k]), 0, 1.6, 0));
      car.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.05, 2.4, 6), metal, 0, 3, -0.5));
      car.userData.v = new THREE.Vector2(Math.cos(k * 1.7) * 2.2, Math.sin(k * 1.7) * 2.2);
      car.position.set((k - 1.5) * 2.4, 0, (k % 2 ? 1 : -1) * 1.5);
      g.add(car); cars.push(car);
    }
    animators.push(dt => cars.forEach(c => {
      const v = c.userData.v; c.position.x += v.x * dt; c.position.z += v.y * dt;
      if (Math.abs(c.position.x) > 4.6) { v.x *= -1; c.position.x = Math.sign(c.position.x) * 4.6; }
      if (Math.abs(c.position.z) > 3.6) { v.y *= -1; c.position.z = Math.sign(c.position.z) * 3.6; }
      c.rotation.y = Math.atan2(v.x, v.y);
    }));
    p.h = 13; return g;
  },
  coaster(p) {
    const g = new THREE.Group(); pad(g, p.hex);
    const pts = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < 12; k++) {
      const a = k / 12 * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = 5.6 + Math.sin(a * 2) * 1.2;
      pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(Math.cos(a) * r, 3 + Math.max(0, Math.sin(a * 2 + 0.6)) * 7 + (k === 3 ? 3 : 0), Math.sin(a) * r));
    }
    const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts, true);
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 160, 0.32, 8, true), M(0xF87171)));
    const off = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts.map(v => new THREE.Vector3(v.x * 1.12, v.y, v.z * 1.12)), true);
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(off, 160, 0.18, 6, true), M(0xFCA5A5)));
    for (let k = 0; k < 24; k++) {
      const v = curve.getPointAt(k / 24);
      g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.15, 0.15, v.y, 6), white, v.x, v.y / 2, v.z));
    }
    const cart = new THREE.Group(); g.add(cart);
    const cc = [0xFACC15, 0x60A5FA, 0x34D399];
    const carts = cc.map(col => { const c = new THREE.Group(); c.add(mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.2, 0.8, 1.5), M(col), 0, 0.6, 0)); g.add(c); return c; });
    let u = 0;
    animators.push(dt => {
      u = (u + dt * 0.07) % 1;
      carts.forEach((c, k) => {
        const t = (u - k * 0.022 + 1) % 1; const pos = curve.getPointAt(t), tan = curve.getTangentAt(t);
        c.position.copy(pos); c.lookAt(pos.clone().add(tan));
      });
    });
    p.h = 18; return g;
  },
  observatory(p) {
    const g = new THREE.Group(); pad(g, p.hex);
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(4.2, 4.6, 5, 32), M(0xEEF2FF), 0, 3, 0));
    g.add(mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(4.3, 32, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), M(0x818CF8), 0, 5.5, 0));
    put(g, mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.2, 4.6, 8.8), M(0x4338CA), 0, 7.2, 0)).scale.set(1, 1, 0.98);
    const scope = mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.9, 5, 16), M(0xFACC15), 0, 9.4, 1.4); scope.rotation.x = -0.7; g.add(scope);
    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) { const a = k / 6 * Math.PI * 2; put(g, mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.9, 1.2, 0.2), windowMat, Math.sin(a) * 4.25, 3.2, Math.cos(a) * 4.25)).rotation.y = a; }
    const star = mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(1.3), new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: 0xFDE047, emissive: 0xFACC15, emissiveIntensity: 0.4 }), 0, 15, 0);
    g.add(star);
    animators.push((dt, t) => { star.rotation.y += dt * 1.2; star.position.y = 15 + Math.sin(t * 1.6) * 0.6; });
    p.h = 19; return g;
  }
};
const pavGroups = [];
PAV.forEach(p => {
  const g = B[p.type](p);
  g.position.copy(p.pos);
  g.rotation.y = Math.atan2(-p.pos.x, -p.pos.z);
  g.traverse(o => { o.userData.pav = p.id; });
  scene.add(g); pavGroups.push(g);
});

// --- 入口大門 ---
function textTexture(lines, opt = {}) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = opt.w || 1024; c.height = opt.h || 256;
  const x = c.getContext('2d');
  x.fillStyle = opt.bg || '#FFFBF0'; x.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
  x.strokeStyle = opt.border || '#5BB334'; x.lineWidth = 18; x.strokeRect(9, 9, c.width - 18, c.height - 18);
  x.textAlign = 'center'; x.textBaseline = 'middle';
  lines.forEach((l, k) => { x.fillStyle = l.c; x.font = `${l.w || 900} ${l.s}px "Huninn","Noto Sans TC","Microsoft JhengHei",sans-serif`; x.fillText(l.t, c.width / 2, l.y); });
  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.anisotropy = 4; return t;
}
const gate = new THREE.Group(); gate.position.set(0, 0, 57); scene.add(gate);
[-5.5, 5.5].forEach((x, k) => {
  gate.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.8, 0.9, 9, 16), M(k ? 0xF9A8D4 : 0x93C5FD), x, 4.5, 0));
  gate.add(mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.1, 16, 16), M(0xFACC15), x, 9.6, 0));
});
let gateTex = textTexture([{ t: 'SWSH AI 自學指令島', s: 96, y: 112, c: '#166534' }, { t: '四維高中 圖書資訊中心', s: 48, y: 196, c: '#B45309', w: 700 }]);
const gateSign = mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(12, 3, 0.5), [white, white, white, white, new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ map: gateTex }), white], 0, 8, 0);
gate.add(gateSign);
document.fonts && document.fonts.ready.then(() => {
  gateTex = textTexture([{ t: 'SWSH AI 自學指令島', s: 96, y: 112, c: '#166534' }, { t: '四維高中 圖書資訊中心', s: 48, y: 196, c: '#B45309', w: 700 }]);
  gateSign.material[4].map = gateTex; gateSign.material[4].needsUpdate = true;
});

// --- 中央噴泉 ---
const fountain = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(fountain);
fountain.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(4.2, 4.5, 1, 32), white, 0, 0.5, 0));
fountain.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(3.7, 3.7, 0.4, 32), M(0x93D8F2), 0, 0.85, 0, false));
fountain.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5, 0.7, 3, 12), white, 0, 2, 0));
fountain.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.8, 0.9, 0.6, 24), white, 0, 3.6, 0));
const spray = mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.4, 2.4, 16, 1, true), new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: 0xBAE6FD, transparent: true, opacity: 0.7, side: THREE.DoubleSide }), 0, 5.1, 0, false);
spray.rotation.x = Math.PI; fountain.add(spray);
animators.push((dt, t) => { spray.scale.y = 1 + Math.sin(t * 4) * 0.15; });

const rainbow = new THREE.Group(); rainbow.position.set(0, -2, -92); scene.add(rainbow);

  c.position.set(Math.cos(a) * r, 42 + Math.random() * 14, Math.sin(a) * r - 10);

}

});

const treeCols = [0x86EFAC, 0x6EE7B7, 0xBBF7D0, 0xF9A8D4, 0xC4B5FD, 0xFDE68A, 0xA7F3D0];
const trunk = M(0xB98B5E);
const okSpot = (x, z, minPav) => {
  const r = Math.hypot(x, z);
  if (r > 58 || r < 26.5) return false;
  if (z > 22 && Math.abs(x) < 6) return false;
  if (PAV.some(p => Math.hypot(p.pos.x - x, p.pos.z - z) < minPav)) return false;
  if (PAV.some(p => { const d = new THREE.Vector2(x, z); const a = new THREE.Vector2(p.door.x, p.door.z), b = new THREE.Vector2(p.pos.x * 0.7, p.pos.z * 0.7); return d.distanceTo(a) < 4 || d.distanceTo(b) < 4; })) return false;
  return true;
};
let placed = 0, tries = 0;
while (placed < 90 && tries < 2000) {
  tries++;
  const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, r = 26 + Math.random() * 33, x = Math.cos(a) * r, z = Math.sin(a) * r;
  if (!okSpot(x, z, 11)) continue;
  const s = 0.7 + Math.random() * 0.8, col = M(treeCols[placed % treeCols.length]);
  const t = new THREE.Group(); t.position.set(x, 0, z); t.scale.setScalar(s);
  t.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.35, 0.45, 2, 8), trunk, 0, 1, 0));
  if (placed % 3) t.add(mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.9, 4.2, 10), col, 0, 4, 0));
  else t.add(mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.9, 12, 10), col, 0, 3.6, 0));
  scene.add(t); placed++;
}
const flowerGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.28, 6, 6);

const fcol = [0xF472B6, 0xFACC15, 0xFFFFFF, 0xA78BFA, 0xFB923C], dummy = new THREE.Object3D(), tmpC = new THREE.Color();
for (let k = 0, n = 0; n < 260 && k < 3000; k++) {
  const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, r = 12 + Math.random() * 47, x = Math.cos(a) * r, z = Math.sin(a) * r;
  if ((r > 18.5 && r < 25) || (z > 22 && Math.abs(x) < 4) || PAV.some(p => Math.hypot(p.pos.x - x, p.pos.z - z) < 9)) continue;
  dummy.position.set(x, 0.25, z); dummy.updateMatrix(); flowers.setMatrixAt(n, dummy.matrix); flowers.setColorAt(n, tmpC.set(fcol[n % 5])); n++;
}
scene.add(flowers);

}

// ===================== 角色 =====================
function avatarTexture(src, ring) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = 256;
  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
  const im = new Image();
  im.onload = () => {
    const x = c.getContext('2d');
    x.clearRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
    x.beginPath(); x.arc(128, 128, 124, 0, Math.PI * 2); x.fillStyle = ring; x.fill();
    x.beginPath(); x.arc(128, 128, 112, 0, Math.PI * 2); x.fillStyle = '#fff'; x.fill();
    x.save(); x.beginPath(); x.arc(128, 128, 106, 0, Math.PI * 2); x.clip(); x.drawImage(im, 22, 22, 212, 212); x.restore();
    tex.needsUpdate = true;
  };
  im.src = src; return tex;
}
const shadowTex = (() => { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = 64; const x = c.getContext('2d'); const g = x.createRadialGradient(32, 32, 2, 32, 32, 30); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(30,41,59,.35)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(30,41,59,0)'); x.fillStyle = g; x.fillRect(0, 0, 64, 64); return new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); })();
function makeChar(id) {
  const c = CHARS[id];
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  const sh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(3.4, 3.4), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: shadowTex, transparent: true, depthWrite: false }));
  sh.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; sh.position.y = 0.05; g.add(sh);
  const body = new THREE.Group(); g.add(body);
  body.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.55, 0.85, 1.7, 16), M(c.body), 0, 1.15, 0));
  body.add(mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.55, 16, 12), M(c.body), 0, 2, 0));
  body.add(mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.86, 0.86, 0.35, 16), M(id === 'pijbo' ? 0xC08A55 : 0x334155), 0, 0.45, 0));
  const head = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: avatarTexture(IMG[id], c.c) }));
  head.scale.set(3, 3, 1); head.position.y = 3.6; g.add(head);
  g.userData = { id, head, body, phase: Math.random() * 10 };
  return g;
}
let player = null;
const npcs = [];
function spawn() {
  if (player) scene.remove(player);
  npcs.splice(0).forEach(n => scene.remove(n.obj));
  player = makeChar(state.char); player.position.set(0, 0, 30); scene.add(player);
  Object.keys(CHARS).filter(k => k !== state.char).forEach((k, i) => {
    const o = makeChar(k); const a = i * 1.6 + 0.4; o.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 15, 0, Math.sin(a) * 15); scene.add(o);
    npcs.push({ obj: o, id: k, target: null, wait: Math.random() * 2, say: 0, sayText: '' });
  });
  buildTags();
}
applyChar = () => { $('#joy-img').src = IMG[state.char]; $('#joy').style.setProperty('--c', CHARS[state.char].c); if (scene) spawn(); };

const labels = $('#labels');
const plabels = PAV.map(p => {
  const b = document.createElement('button');
  b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'plabel glass';
  b.innerHTML = `<span class="ic" style="background:${p.s};border:2px solid ${p.c}">${p.emoji}</span><span><b>${p.name}</b><small>${p.ride}</small></span>`;
  b.setAttribute('aria-label', `前往 ${p.name}`);
  b.addEventListener('click', () => goTo(p.id));
  labels.appendChild(b); return { el: b, p };
});
let ctags = [];
function buildTags() {
  ctags.forEach(t => { t.tag.remove(); t.say.remove(); });
  ctags = [player, ...npcs.map(n => n.obj)].map((o, k) => {
    const c = CHARS[o.userData.id];
    const tag = document.createElement('div'); tag.className = 'ctag' + (k === 0 ? ' me' : ''); tag.style.setProperty('--c', c.c);
    tag.textContent = k === 0 ? `⭐ 你・${c.name}` : c.name;
    const say = document.createElement('div'); say.className = 'say'; say.style.setProperty('--c', c.c); say.style.opacity = 0;
    labels.appendChild(tag); labels.appendChild(say);
    return { o, tag, say };
  });
}

// ===================== 輸入 =====================
const keys = new Set();
addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (panel.open || !$('#intro').hidden || /INPUT|TEXTAREA/.test(document.activeElement.tagName)) return;
  const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
  if (['w', 'a', 's', 'd', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright'].includes(k)) { keys.add(k); moveTarget = null; e.preventDefault(); }
  if ((k === 'e' || k === 'enter') && nearPav) openPanel(nearPav.id);
});
addEventListener('keyup', e => keys.delete(e.key.toLowerCase()));
addEventListener('blur', () => keys.clear());

const joy = $('#joy'), knob = joy.querySelector('.knob');
const joyV = new THREE.Vector2();
let joyId = null;
joy.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { joyId = e.pointerId; joy.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); joy.classList.add('active'); moveTarget = null; joyMove(e); });
joy.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { if (e.pointerId === joyId) joyMove(e); });
const joyEnd = e => { if (e.pointerId !== joyId) return; joyId = null; joyV.set(0, 0); knob.style.transform = ''; joy.classList.remove('active'); };
joy.addEventListener('pointerup', joyEnd); joy.addEventListener('pointercancel', joyEnd);
function joyMove(e) {
  const r = joy.getBoundingClientRect(), max = r.width * 0.32;
  let dx = e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2), dy = e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2);
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy); if (d > max) { dx *= max / d; dy *= max / d; }
  knob.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px)`;
  joyV.set(dx / max, -dy / max);
}

let camMode = 'follow', yaw = 0, dist = narrow() ? 36 : 30, birdDist = 92;
let moveTarget = null, onArrive = null;
const marker = mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(0.8, 1.2, 24), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xFACC15, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9 }), 0, 0.08, 0, false);
marker.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; marker.visible = false; scene.add(marker);

const ray = new THREE.Raycaster(), ndc = new THREE.Vector2();
let drag = null;
const cvs = renderer.domElement;
cvs.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { drag = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, moved: false, id: e.pointerId }; cvs.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); });
cvs.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
  const dx = e.clientX - drag.x;
  if (!drag.moved && Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(e.clientY - drag.y) > 6) drag.moved = true;
  if (drag.moved) { yaw -= dx * 0.006; drag.x = e.clientX; drag.y = e.clientY; }
});
cvs.addEventListener('pointerup', e => {
  if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
  const wasDrag = drag.moved; drag = null;
  if (wasDrag) return;
  ndc.set(e.clientX / innerWidth * 2 - 1, -(e.clientY / innerHeight) * 2 + 1);
  ray.setFromCamera(ndc, camera);
  const hit = ray.intersectObjects(pavGroups, true)[0];
  if (hit && hit.object.userData.pav) return goTo(hit.object.userData.pav);
  const g = ray.intersectObject(island)[0] || ray.intersectObject(ring)[0];
  if (g) walkTo(g.point.x, g.point.z, null);
});
cvs.addEventListener('wheel', e => { e.preventDefault(); zoom(e.deltaY > 0 ? 1 : -1); }, { passive: false });
function zoom(dir) { if (camMode === 'follow') dist = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(dist + dir * 3, 12, 54); else birdDist = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(birdDist + dir * 8, 50, 140); }
$('#z-in').addEventListener('click', () => zoom(-1));
$('#z-out').addEventListener('click', () => zoom(1));

let route = [];
function walkTo(x, z, cb) {
  route = [];
  const s = player.position, dx = x - s.x, dz = z - s.z, L2 = dx * dx + dz * dz;
  const u = L2 ? THREE.MathUtils.clamp(-(s.x * dx + s.z * dz) / L2, 0, 1) : 0;
  const cx = s.x + dx * u, cz = s.z + dz * u;
  if (u > 0 && u < 1 && Math.hypot(cx, cz) < 7) {
    let px = -dz, pz = dx; const pl = Math.hypot(px, pz) || 1; px /= pl; pz /= pl;
    if (px * cx + pz * cz < 0) { px = -px; pz = -pz; }
    route.push(new THREE.Vector3(px * 9, 0, pz * 9));
  }
  route.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, 0, z));
  moveTarget = route.shift(); onArrive = cb;
  marker.position.set(x, 0.08, z); marker.visible = true;
}
goTo = id => {
  const p = byId[id];
  if (!player) return openPanel(id);
  if (player.position.distanceTo(p.door) < 3) return openPanel(id);
  walkTo(p.door.x, p.door.z, () => openPanel(id));
  toast(`${CHARS[state.char].name} 正前往 ${p.emoji} ${p.name}`);
};

$('#t-view').addEventListener('click', e => {
  camMode = camMode === 'follow' ? 'bird' : 'follow';
  const b = e.currentTarget; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', camMode === 'bird');
  b.innerHTML = camMode === 'bird' ? '🎥<span class="t">跟隨</span>' : '🦅<span class="t">鳥瞰</span>';
});
let night = false;
$('#t-night').addEventListener('click', e => {
  night = !night; const b = e.currentTarget; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', night);
  b.innerHTML = night ? '☀️<span class="t">白天</span>' : '🌙<span class="t">夜晚</span>';
  const P = night ? NIGHT : DAY;
  scene.background.set(P.sky); scene.fog.color.set(P.fog);
  hemi.intensity = night ? 0.38 : 0.52; hemi.color.set(night ? 0x9AA8FF : 0xffffff); hemi.groundColor.set(night ? 0x334155 : 0x9CC98A);
  sun.intensity = night ? 0.25 : 0.62; sun.color.set(night ? 0xA8B8FF : 0xFFF6E5);
  lampMat.emissiveIntensity = night ? 1.4 : 0.15; windowMat.emissiveIntensity = night ? 1.1 : 0;
  lampLights.forEach(L => (L.intensity = night ? 1.6 : 0));
  stars.material.opacity = night ? 0.9 : 0; cloudMat.opacity = night ? 0.35 : 0.95;
  rainbow.visible = !night;
});

// ===================== 小地圖 =====================
const mini = $('#mini-c'), mx = mini.getContext('2d');
const MW = mini.width, MH = mini.height, MS = MH / 2 / 66;
const toMini = (x, z) => [MW / 2 + x * MS, MH / 2 + z * MS];
function drawMini() {
  mx.clearRect(0, 0, MW, MH);
  mx.fillStyle = night ? '#2B3570' : '#BFE6F5'; mx.fillRect(0, 0, MW, MH);
  mx.fillStyle = '#F6E5B8'; mx.beginPath(); mx.arc(MW / 2, MH / 2, 65 * MS, 0, 7); mx.fill();
  mx.fillStyle = night ? '#6C8F63' : '#C5E8A7'; mx.beginPath(); mx.arc(MW / 2, MH / 2, 61 * MS, 0, 7); mx.fill();
  mx.strokeStyle = '#F7E8CB'; mx.lineWidth = 5.5 * MS; mx.beginPath(); mx.arc(MW / 2, MH / 2, 21.7 * MS, 0, 7); mx.stroke();
  mx.lineWidth = 6 * MS; mx.beginPath(); mx.moveTo(...toMini(0, 22)); mx.lineTo(...toMini(0, 56)); mx.stroke();
  mx.font = '22px sans-serif'; mx.textAlign = 'center'; mx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  PAV.forEach(p => {
    mx.strokeStyle = '#F7E8CB'; mx.lineWidth = 4.5 * MS; mx.beginPath(); mx.moveTo(...toMini(p.door.x * 0.8, p.door.z * 0.8)); mx.lineTo(...toMini(p.pos.x, p.pos.z)); mx.stroke();
    const [x, y] = toMini(p.pos.x, p.pos.z);
    mx.fillStyle = state.visited.includes(p.id) ? '#FEF08A' : '#ffffff'; mx.beginPath(); mx.arc(x, y, 15, 0, 7); mx.fill();
    mx.strokeStyle = p.c; mx.lineWidth = 3; mx.stroke();
    mx.fillText(p.emoji, x, y + 1);
  });
  npcs.forEach(n => { const [x, y] = toMini(n.obj.position.x, n.obj.position.z); mx.fillStyle = CHARS[n.id].c; mx.beginPath(); mx.arc(x, y, 5, 0, 7); mx.fill(); });
  if (player) {
    const [x, y] = toMini(player.position.x, player.position.z);
    mx.save(); mx.translate(x, y); mx.rotate(-yaw);
    mx.fillStyle = CHARS[state.char].i; mx.strokeStyle = '#fff'; mx.lineWidth = 3;
    mx.beginPath(); mx.moveTo(0, -12); mx.lineTo(9, 9); mx.lineTo(0, 4); mx.lineTo(-9, 9); mx.closePath(); mx.fill(); mx.stroke();
    mx.restore();
  }
}
mini.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const r = mini.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * MW - MW / 2) / MS, z = ((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height * MH - MH / 2) / MS;
  const hitP = PAV.find(p => Math.hypot(p.pos.x - x, p.pos.z - z) < 9);
  if (hitP) return goTo(hitP.id);
  if (Math.hypot(x, z) < 57) walkTo(x, z, null);
});

// ===================== 迴圈 =====================
const clock = new THREE.Clock();
const camTarget = new THREE.Vector3(0, 2, 30);
camera.position.set(0, 30, 80);
let nearPav = null;
const v3 = new THREE.Vector3();
function collide(pos, rad) {
  const r = Math.hypot(pos.x, pos.z);
  if (r > 56) { pos.x *= 56 / r; pos.z *= 56 / r; }
  if (r < 5) { const k = 5 / (r || 1); pos.x *= k; pos.z *= k; }
  PAV.forEach(p => {
    const dx = pos.x - p.pos.x, dz = pos.z - p.pos.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz), m = 8.2 + rad;
    if (d < m) { pos.x = p.pos.x + dx / (d || 1) * m; pos.z = p.pos.z + dz / (d || 1) * m; }
  });
}
function stepChar(o, dir, speed, dt, t) {
  const moving = dir.lengthSq() > 0.0001;
  if (moving) { o.position.x += dir.x * speed * dt; o.position.z += dir.y * speed * dt; collide(o.position, 0.8); }
  const u = o.userData, ph = t * 11 + u.phase;
  u.head.position.y = 3.6 + (moving ? Math.abs(Math.sin(ph)) * 0.35 : Math.sin(t * 2 + u.phase) * 0.08);
  u.body.rotation.z = moving ? Math.sin(ph) * 0.08 : 0;
  return moving;
}
function project(obj, yOff, el, dy = 0) {
  v3.set(obj.x, obj.y + yOff, obj.z).project(camera);
  if (v3.z > 1 || v3.z < -1) { el.style.display = 'none'; return false; }
  el.style.display = '';
  const x = (v3.x + 1) / 2 * innerWidth, y = (1 - v3.y) / 2 * innerHeight;
  el.style.transform = `translate(${x - el.offsetWidth / 2}px, ${y - el.offsetHeight - dy}px)`;
  return true;
}
let miniTick = 0;
function frame() {
  const dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.05), t = clock.elapsedTime;
  animators.forEach(f => f(reduceMotion ? dt * 0.3 : dt, t));

  // 玩家輸入
  const fwd = new THREE.Vector2(-Math.sin(yaw), -Math.cos(yaw)), right = new THREE.Vector2(Math.cos(yaw), -Math.sin(yaw));
  let ix = joyV.x, iy = joyV.y;
  if (keys.has('w') || keys.has('arrowup')) iy += 1;
  if (keys.has('s') || keys.has('arrowdown')) iy -= 1;
  if (keys.has('a') || keys.has('arrowleft')) ix -= 1;
  if (keys.has('d') || keys.has('arrowright')) ix += 1;
  const dir = new THREE.Vector2();
  if (ix || iy) { dir.addScaledVector(fwd, iy).addScaledVector(right, ix); if (dir.length() > 1) dir.normalize(); moveTarget = null; marker.visible = false; }
  else if (moveTarget) {
    const dx = moveTarget.x - player.position.x, dz = moveTarget.z - player.position.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    if (d < 0.6) { if (route.length) moveTarget = route.shift(); else { moveTarget = null; marker.visible = false; const cb = onArrive; onArrive = null; cb && cb(); } }
    else dir.set(dx / d, dz / d);
  }
  const before = player.position.clone();
  stepChar(player, dir, 13, dt, t);
  if (moveTarget && player.position.distanceTo(before) < 13 * dt * 0.15 && dir.lengthSq() > 0) { moveTarget = null; marker.visible = false; onArrive = null; }
  marker.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(t * 6) * 0.15);

  // NPC
  npcs.forEach(n => {
    const o = n.obj;
    if (!n.target) { n.wait -= dt; if (n.wait <= 0) { const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, r = 8 + Math.random() * 16; n.target = new THREE.Vector2(Math.cos(a) * r, Math.sin(a) * r); } }
    const d2 = new THREE.Vector2();
    if (n.target) {
      const dx = n.target.x - o.position.x, dz = n.target.y - o.position.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
      if (d < 0.5) { n.target = null; n.wait = 1.5 + Math.random() * 3; } else d2.set(dx / d, dz / d);
    }
    const prev = o.position.clone();
    stepChar(o, d2, 3.6, dt, t);
    if (n.target && o.position.distanceTo(prev) < 3.6 * dt * 0.3) { n.stuck = (n.stuck || 0) + dt; if (n.stuck > 1) { n.target = null; n.stuck = 0; n.wait = 0.5; } } else n.stuck = 0;
    n.say -= dt;
    if (n.say < -6 - Math.random() * 6 && o.position.distanceTo(player.position) < 16) { n.say = 3.2; n.sayText = NPC_LINES[n.id][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)]; }
  });

  // 靠近指令館
  let best = null, bd = 12;
  PAV.forEach(p => { const d = Math.hypot(player.position.x - p.door.x, player.position.z - p.door.z); if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = p; } });
  if (best !== nearPav) {
    nearPav = best;
    const nb = $('#near');
    if (best) { $('#near-t').textContent = `${best.emoji} ${best.name}`; nb.style.setProperty('--c', best.c); nb.classList.add('show'); }
    else nb.classList.remove('show');
  }

  // 相機
  const k = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 3.5);
  let desired, look;
  if (camMode === 'follow') {
    desired = new THREE.Vector3(player.position.x + Math.sin(yaw) * dist, dist * 0.72 + 3, player.position.z + Math.cos(yaw) * dist);
    look = new THREE.Vector3(player.position.x, 3, player.position.z);
  } else {
    desired = new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(yaw) * birdDist * 0.75, birdDist * 0.85, Math.cos(yaw) * birdDist * 0.75);
    look = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -2);
  }
  camera.position.lerp(desired, reduceMotion ? 1 : k);
  camTarget.lerp(look, reduceMotion ? 1 : k);
  camera.lookAt(camTarget);

  renderer.render(scene, camera);

  // 標籤
  plabels.forEach(({ el, p }) => project(new THREE.Vector3(p.pos.x, 0, p.pos.z), p.h, el));
  ctags.forEach(({ o, tag, say }, i) => {
    const vis = project(o.position, 5.4, tag);
    const n = i > 0 ? npcs[i - 1] : null;
    if (n && n.say > 0 && vis) { say.textContent = n.sayText; say.style.opacity = 1; project(o.position, 5.4, say, 26); }
    else say.style.opacity = 0;
  });
  if ((miniTick = (miniTick + 1) % 3) === 0) drawMini();
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
$('#near-go').addEventListener('click', () => nearPav && openPanel(nearPav.id));
addEventListener('resize', () => { camera.fov = narrow() ? 60 : 48; camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight; camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight); });
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
